# 1. GOSPEL 444: THE FOUNDATION



## 1.1 WHAT IS GOSPEL 444?



Gospel 444 is NOT just branding.

Gospel 444 is NOT just aesthetics.

Gospel 444 is NOT optional.



**GOSPEL 444 IS:**

- The governing law of ALL NET visual and technical systems

- Brand identity + Security protocol + UX consistency + AI governance

- The aesthetic signature of the NET Universe

- A complete philosophical and operational framework

- The constraint system that prevents drift and chaos



## 1.2 WHY "444"?



- Represents **foundation and balance** (numerology)

- Easy to remember and reference

- Distinctive brand element

- Symbolizes: **Four Pillars** (pedagogy), **Four Agents** (AI system), **Four-layer depth** (visual)



## 1.3 THE FOUR PILLARS OF GOSPEL 444



### PILLAR I: AESTHETICS (This Bible)

The visual law: colors, typography, layout, icons, glassmorphism



### PILLAR II: STABILITY (Technical)

Vanilla JS only, no inline handlers, event binding, web workers, security



### PILLAR III: PEDAGOGY (Teaching)

Confidence first, natural acquisition, Four Pillars method, 1-hour daily



### PILLAR IV: COMMUNITY (Social)

Buddy system, monthly events, networking, Tiger/Mouse archetypes



## 1.4 THE VISUAL PHILOSOPHY



**"Glow-Behind-Glass"** — A 4-layer visual architecture:



1. **Deep Dark Background** — Foundation (#0f0f1a)

2. **Animated Organic Blobs** — Ambient depth (60-80px blur)

3. **Glass Panels** — Semi-transparent containers (blur ≥12px)

4. **Content with Glow** — Interactive elements with neon halos



**This creates:**

- Depth and dimensionality

- Ultra-low energy design (dark mode default)

- Professional, premium feel

- Distinctive brand signature

- Reduced eye strain

- Futuristic aesthetic



---



# 2. COLOR PALETTE: THE MANDATES



## 2.1 PRIMARY COLORS (MANDATORY)



### NEON PURPLE

```

HEX: #a855f7

RGB: (168, 85, 247)

HSL: (277°, 91%, 65%)

```



**USAGE:**

- All interactive elements (buttons, links, CTAs)

- Primary brand color

- Active states and selections

- Text glows and emphasis

- Hero sections and headlines

- Navigation highlights

- Form focus states



**PSYCHOLOGY:**

- Creativity, innovation, transformation

- Premium, sophisticated

- Memorable, distinctive

- High contrast on dark backgrounds



---



### GOLD (ACCENT)

```

HEX: #f59e0b

RGB: (245, 158, 11)

HSL: (38°, 92%, 50%)

```



**USAGE:**

- Secondary accents

- Success states and celebrations

- Rewards and achievements

- Important highlights

- Call-to-action secondary

- Complementary glows

- Progress indicators



**PSYCHOLOGY:**

- Success, achievement, value

- Warmth, positivity

- Luxury, premium quality

- Energy, enthusiasm



---



### DEEP DARK (BACKGROUND)

```

HEX: #0f0f1a

RGB: (15, 15, 26)

HSL: (240°, 27%, 8%)

```



**USAGE:**

- Primary background (all pages, all products)

- Creates depth and contrast

- Foundation for glow effects

- Reduces eye strain

- Professional appearance

- Ultra-low energy consumption



**PSYCHOLOGY:**

- Sophistication, premium

- Focus, concentration

- Calm, comfortable

- Modern, tech-forward



---



## 2.2 UI COLORS (GLASS & TEXT)



### GLASS PANEL BACKGROUND

```

RGBA: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.7)

Alternative: rgba(38, 38, 60, 0.6)

```



**USAGE:**

- Glassmorphism panels

- Content containers

- Modal backgrounds

- Card elements

- Navigation bars

- Sidebar backgrounds



**PROPERTIES:**

- Semi-transparent

- Always paired with `backdrop-filter: blur(12px)` minimum

- Creates depth separation

- Allows blob glow to show through



---



### GLASS BORDER

```

RGBA: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2)

```



**USAGE:**

- Borders on glass panels

- Subtle container outlines

- Dividers and separators

- Focus rings (brighter: 0.4-0.6 opacity)



---



### TEXT PRIMARY

```

HEX: #ffffff

RGBA: rgba(255, 255, 255, 1)

```



**USAGE:**

- Main body text

- Headers and titles

- High-priority content

- Maximum readability



---



### TEXT SECONDARY

```

RGBA: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7)

Alternative: rgba(229, 231, 235, 0.8) (#e5e7eb with opacity)

```



**USAGE:**

- Descriptive text

- Supporting content

- Less critical information

- Subtle labels



---



### TEXT TERTIARY

```

RGBA: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5)

```



**USAGE:**

- Placeholders

- Disabled text

- Timestamps

- Metadata



---



## 2.3 SEMANTIC COLORS



### SUCCESS

```

HEX: #22c55e (Green)

```



**USAGE:** Positive feedback, completed tasks, success messages



### ERROR

```

HEX: #ef4444 (Red)

```



**USAGE:** Error states, warnings, critical alerts



### WARNING

```

HEX: #f59e0b (Gold - reuse)

```



**USAGE:** Caution, important notices, attention needed



### INFO

```

HEX: #3b82f6 (Blue)

```



**USAGE:** Informational messages, neutral notifications



**NOTE:** Blue is ONLY used for semantic info states, NEVER as a primary brand color or accent.



---



## 2.4 THE BLUE PROHIBITION



### ⚠️ CRITICAL: NO BLUE AS PRIMARY COLOR



**ABSOLUTELY FORBIDDEN:**

- Blue as primary brand color

- Blue in hero sections

- Blue for CTAs or buttons (except info states)

- Blue backgrounds

- Blue accent glows



**EXCEPTION:**

Blue is PERMITTED only for:

- Semantic "info" states

- Error message icons (standardized UI)

- Antagonists in StoryFlow narratives (represents "Noise")



**WHY THIS RULE EXISTS:**

- Blue is generic tech aesthetic

- Blue breaks brand distinctiveness

- Blue dilutes neon purple signature

- Blue signals competitor products



**Violation = F-GRADE FAILURE**



---



## 2.5 COLOR USAGE EXAMPLES



### Correct Usage ✅

```css

/* Primary CTA Button */

.cta-button {

background: linear-gradient(135deg, #a855f7, #ec4899);

color: #ffffff;

box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);

}



/* Glass Panel */

.glass-card {

background: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.7);

border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);

backdrop-filter: blur(12px);

}



/* Success State */

.success-message {

background: rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.1);

border-left: 3px solid #22c55e;

color: #22c55e;

}

```



### Incorrect Usage ❌

```css

/* WRONG: Blue as primary */

.button-primary {

background: #3b82f6; /* VIOLATION */

}



/* WRONG: Light background */

.container {

background: #ffffff; /* VIOLATION */

}



/* WRONG: No backdrop blur */

.glass-panel {

background: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.7);

/* Missing: backdrop-filter: blur(12px); */

}

```



---



# 3. TYPOGRAPHY: THE HIERARCHY



## 3.1 FONT FAMILIES



### PRIMARY: INTER

```css

font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;

```



**SOURCE:** Google Fonts

**WEIGHTS:** 400 (Regular), 600 (Semi-Bold), 700 (Bold), 900 (Black)



**USAGE:**

- All UI text

- Body content

- Buttons and CTAs

- Navigation menus

- Form labels and inputs

- Marketing copy

- Headers and titles



**WHY INTER:**

- Exceptional readability at all sizes

- Professional, modern aesthetic

- Variable font support

- Excellent at small sizes

- Neutral, versatile

- Web-optimized



---



### SECONDARY: JETBRAINS MONO

```css

font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', 'Courier New', monospace;

```



**SOURCE:** Google Fonts

**WEIGHTS:** 400 (Regular), 700 (Bold)



**USAGE:**

- Code snippets and technical content

- Scores and metrics

- Technical logs

- API documentation

- Developer-facing content

- Data displays

- Fixed-width requirements



**WHY JETBRAINS MONO:**

- Designed for developers

- Excellent code readability

- Distinctive character shapes

- Ligature support

- Professional coding aesthetic



---



## 3.2 FONT SIZE SCALE



### HERO TYPOGRAPHY

```css

font-size: clamp(3rem, 10vw, 8rem);

line-height: 1.1;

font-weight: 900;

```



**USAGE:**

- Landing page heroes

- Major section headers

- Promotional headlines

- Attention-grabbing titles



**PROPERTIES:**

- Massive scale (48px - 128px)

- Ultra-bold (900 weight)

- Tight line height

- Often uses gradient text effect

- Responsive scaling with clamp()



---



### HEADING SCALE

```css

h1: 2.25rem (36px) — Page titles

h2: 1.875rem (30px) — Major sections

h3: 1.5rem (24px) — Subsections

h4: 1.25rem (20px) — Minor sections

h5: 1.125rem (18px) — Small headers

h6: 1rem (16px) — Tiny headers

```



**WEIGHTS:**

- h1-h2: 700-900 (Bold to Black)

- h3-h4: 600-700 (Semi-Bold to Bold)

- h5-h6: 600 (Semi-Bold)



---



### BODY TEXT

```css

font-size: 1rem (16px)

line-height: 1.6

font-weight: 400

```



**USAGE:** Standard paragraph text, descriptions, content



---



### SMALL TEXT

```css

font-size: 0.875rem (14px) — Small labels, metadata

font-size: 0.75rem (12px) — Tiny text, captions, footnotes

```



---



## 3.3 TEXT EFFECTS



### GRADIENT TEXT (Hero/Emphasis)

```css

.gradient-text {

background: linear-gradient(135deg, #a855f7 0%, #ec4899 50%, #f59e0b 100%);

-webkit-background-clip: text;

-webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;

background-clip: text;

}

```



**USAGE:** Hero headlines, major CTAs, special emphasis



---



### GLOW TEXT (Interactive)

```css

.glow-text {

color: #a855f7;

text-shadow:

0 0 10px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5),

0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3),

0 0 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);

}

```



**USAGE:** Buttons, links, active states, hover effects



---



### CODE TEXT

```css

.code-text {

font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;

background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);

padding: 0.2em 0.4em;

border-radius: 4px;

font-size: 0.9em;

}

```



**USAGE:** Inline code, technical terms, API references



---



## 3.4 TYPOGRAPHY RULES



### DO's ✅

- Use Inter for 95% of all text

- Maintain consistent scale (use rem, not px)

- Keep line-height between 1.4-1.8 for body text

- Use gradient text for major headlines

- Add glow effects to interactive text

- Keep hierarchy clear (3-4 levels max per page)

- Use bold weights (700+) for emphasis



### DON'Ts ❌

- Don't use more than 2 font families per product

- Don't use font sizes below 12px (0.75rem)

- Don't use ALL CAPS for body text

- Don't use italic as primary emphasis (use bold instead)

- Don't mix too many font weights on one page

- Don't use decorative or script fonts (breaks professionalism)



---



# 4. LAYOUT SYSTEMS: THE ARCHITECTURE



## 4.1 THE 4-LAYER SYSTEM



### LAYER 1: DARK FOUNDATION

```css

body {

background: #0f0f1a;

/* Optional gradient variation: */

background: linear-gradient(180deg, #0a0a0f 0%, #0f0f1a 100%);

}

```



**PURPOSE:** Base canvas, provides depth, reduces eye strain



---



### LAYER 2: ORGANIC BLOBS (AMBIENT DEPTH)

```css

body::before {

content: '';

position: fixed;

top: -10%;

left: -10%;

width: 400px;

height: 400px;

background: radial-gradient(circle, #a855f7, transparent 60%);

filter: blur(80px);

opacity: 0.3;

z-index: -1;

animation: float 20s infinite alternate;

pointer-events: none;

}



body::after {

content: '';

position: fixed;

bottom: -15%;

right: -10%;

width: 500px;

height: 500px;

background: radial-gradient(circle, #f59e0b, transparent 60%);

filter: blur(80px);

opacity: 0.2;

z-index: -1;

animation: float 25s infinite alternate-reverse;

pointer-events: none;

}



@keyframes float {

0%, 100% { transform: translate(0, 0) scale(1); }

33% { transform: translate(30px, -30px) scale(1.1); }

66% { transform: translate(-20px, 20px) scale(0.9); }

}

```



**PURPOSE:**

- Creates ambient depth and dimension

- Adds visual interest without distraction

- Provides "glow-behind" effect for glass panels

- Subtle animation creates life and energy



**VARIATIONS:**

- Add 3rd blob (pink #ec4899) at center for complex layouts

- Adjust blur (60-100px) based on viewport size

- Vary opacity (0.15-0.35) for different moods



---



### LAYER 3: GLASS PANELS (CONTAINERS)

```css

.glass-panel {

background: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.7);

backdrop-filter: blur(12px);

-webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px); /* Safari */

border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);

border-radius: 16px; /* or 1rem */

box-shadow:

0 8px 32px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4),

0 0 0 1px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1) inset;

padding: 2rem;

transition: all 0.3s ease;

}



.glass-panel:hover {

transform: translateY(-4px);

border-color: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);

box-shadow:

0 12px 48px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5),

0 0 40px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15);

}

```



**PURPOSE:**

- Primary content containers

- Creates depth separation

- Allows blob glow to show through

- Interactive hover states



**VARIATIONS:**

- **Subtle glass:** `backdrop-filter: blur(8px)`, lower opacity

- **Strong glass:** `backdrop-filter: blur(16px)`, higher opacity

- **Large panels:** 24px border-radius

- **Cards:** 12px border-radius



---



### LAYER 4: CONTENT WITH GLOW (INTERACTIVE)

```css

.button-primary {

background: linear-gradient(135deg, #a855f7 0%, #ec4899 100%);

color: #ffffff;

padding: 0.75rem 2rem;

border: none;

border-radius: 12px;

font-weight: 600;

font-size: 1rem;

box-shadow:

0 4px 16px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3),

0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) inset;

transition: all 0.3s ease;

cursor: pointer;

}



.button-primary:hover {

transform: translateY(-2px);

box-shadow:

0 8px 24px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5),

0 0 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3),

0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2) inset;

}



.button-primary:active {

transform: translateY(0);

}

```



**PURPOSE:**

- Interactive elements

- Clear call-to-actions

- Visual hierarchy

- User engagement



---



## 4.2 GLASSMORPHISM SPECIFICATIONS



### MINIMUM REQUIREMENTS (MANDATORY)

1. **Backdrop Blur:** ≥12px (minimum, can be higher)

2. **Semi-transparent Background:** RGBA with 0.5-0.8 alpha

3. **Border:** 1px with RGBA purple at 0.1-0.3 alpha

4. **Border Radius:** 12-24px (smooth, modern curves)

5. **Box Shadow:** Multi-layer for depth



### BLUR VALUES BY USE CASE

- **Small cards/chips:** 8-12px blur

- **Standard panels:** 12-16px blur

- **Large containers:** 16-20px blur

- **Modals/overlays:** 20-24px blur

- **Background blobs:** 60-80px blur (not glassmorphism)



### COMMON MISTAKES TO AVOID ❌

- Blur < 12px (looks cheap, unprofessional)

- Opaque backgrounds (kills the glass effect)

- No border (loses definition)

- Sharp corners (use 12px+ border-radius)

- Forgetting `-webkit-backdrop-filter` (Safari support)



---



## 4.3 SPACING & GRID SYSTEM



### SPACING SCALE (8px BASE)

```css

:root {

--space-xs: 0.5rem; /* 8px */

--space-sm: 0.75rem; /* 12px */

--space-md: 1rem; /* 16px */

--space-lg: 1.5rem; /* 24px */

--space-xl: 2rem; /* 32px */

--space-2xl: 3rem; /* 48px */

--space-3xl: 4rem; /* 64px */

}

```



**USAGE:**

- Padding: Use lg (24px) for panels, md (16px) for cards

- Margins: Use xl (32px) between sections, lg (24px) between elements

- Gaps: Use md (16px) for grids, sm (12px) for dense layouts



---



### GRID SYSTEM

```css

.container {

max-width: 1200px;

margin: 0 auto;

padding: 0 var(--space-lg);

}



.grid {

display: grid;

grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr));

gap: var(--space-xl);

}



.flex-row {

display: flex;

gap: var(--space-md);

align-items: center;

}

```



**BREAKPOINTS:**

- Mobile: < 640px

- Tablet: 640px - 1024px

- Desktop: > 1024px

- Wide: > 1440px



---

## 5.2 ICON SPECIFICATIONS



### SIZE SCALE

```css

--icon-xs: 16px; /* Small inline icons */

--icon-sm: 20px; /* Standard inline */

--icon-lg: 32px; /* Section headers */

--icon-xl: 48px; /* Feature highlights */
```

```css

/* Default state */

.icon {

color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);

transition: color 0.3s ease;

}



/* Interactive state */

.icon-interactive:hover {

color: #a855f7;

filter: drop-shadow(0 0 8px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6));

}

color: #a855f7;

}

color: #22c55e;

}
```

### STROKE WIDTH

- Default: 2px (standard)

- Bold: 2.5px (emphasis)

- Light: 1.5px (subtle)

## 5.3 ICON USAGE RULES



### DO's ✅

- Use consistent size within the same context

- Apply glow effects to interactive icons

- Use semantic colors (green for success, red for error)

- Maintain consistent stroke width across the set

- Use inline SVGs for performance and styling control



### DON'Ts ❌

- Don't mix icon libraries (no Font Awesome, Material, etc.)

- Don't use bitmap/raster icons (PNG, JPG)

- Don't use icons below 16px (becomes unclear)

- Don't apply heavy effects (keep it clean)

- Don't use different visual styles (flat vs. 3D)

# 6. BRAND IDENTITY: THE ELEPHANT STORY



## 6.1 THE CORE NARRATIVE



### THE METAPHOR



**Vietnamese adults learning English are like elephants in chains.**



From childhood, they're told:

- "Your English must be perfect"

- "Don't speak until you know every grammar rule"

- "Mistakes mean you're stupid"



These messages become **invisible chains**. Even when adults want to speak, the chains hold them back. They have the power (like an elephant), but they believe they can't move.



### THE TRANSFORMATION



**NET breaks the chains.**



We show students that:

1. The chains were never real

2. Confidence comes before perfection

3. Natural speaking beats grammar drills

4. They can speak from Day 1



**The elephant realizes it's free. It walks forward.**



---



## 6.2 THE TIGER & MOUSE ARCHETYPES



### 🐯 TIGER VOICE

**CHARACTERISTICS:**

- Loud, confident, bold

- Speaks without fear

- Makes mistakes freely

- Projects energy and presence

- Commands attention

- "I'm here to be heard"



**GOAL:** Every student becomes their Tiger Voice



---



### 🐭 MOUSE VOICE

**CHARACTERISTICS:**

- Quiet, timid, scared

- Whispers or mumbles

- Afraid of mistakes

- Hides their voice

- Apologizes constantly

- "I hope nobody notices me"



**PROBLEM:** Most Vietnamese English learners start as Mouse Voice



**NET MISSION:** Transform Mouse → Tiger



---



## 6.3 THE GRAMMAR PRISON



**THE CONCEPT:**



Traditional Vietnamese English education is a **prison** where students are:

- Locked in grammar rules

- Shamed for mistakes

- Tested on memorization, not communication

- Forced to be perfect before speaking

- Isolated from real conversation



**THE ESCAPE:**



NET is the **key to freedom**:

- Break out of textbook thinking

- Speak naturally from Day 1

- Build confidence through conversation

- Join a community of escapees

- Never go back to prison



---



## 6.4 THE PHOENIX REBIRTH



**THE METAPHOR:**



Learning English with NET is a **rebirth**:

- Old scared self burns away

- New confident speaker emerges

- Transformation is permanent

- You can never un-learn confidence



**VISUAL REPRESENTATION:**

- Phoenix rising from ashes

- Flames of transformation

- Upward movement, growth

- Gold and purple fire (brand colors)



**MESSAGING:**

- "Rise from the ashes of fear"

- "Be reborn as a confident speaker"

- "Your transformation starts now"



---



## 6.5 BRAND PERSONALITY



### ATTRIBUTES

- **Bold** — We challenge traditional methods

- **Empowering** — We build confidence, not shame

- **Real** — We speak truth, no false promises

- **Supportive** — We're with you every step

# 7. VOICE & TONE: THE LANGUAGE



## 7.1 CORE VOICE PRINCIPLES



### 1. SPEAK HUMAN, NOT CORPORATE

❌ "Our innovative pedagogical approach leverages neuroscience-based methodologies..."

✅ "We teach English the way you learned Vietnamese — naturally, through conversation."



### 2. BE BOLD, NOT LOUD

❌ "WE'RE THE #1 BEST ENGLISH SCHOOL IN VIETNAM!!!"

✅ "We don't teach English. We build English speakers."



### 3. CONFIDENCE OVER HYPE

❌ "Transform your English in 7 days with our SECRET METHOD!"

✅ "1 hour a day. 90 days. Real confidence. Guaranteed."



### 4. EMPATHY FIRST

❌ "Why is your English still bad after 10 years?"

✅ "You're not bad at English. The system you learned in was broken."



### 5. SHOW, DON'T JUST TELL

❌ "We have amazing teachers!"

✅ "Our teachers interrupt you mid-sentence — not to correct, but to celebrate your courage."



---



## 7.2 TONE VARIATIONS BY CONTEXT



### MARKETING (BOLD & MOTIVATING)

- Short, punchy sentences

- Active voice

- Direct address ("you")

- Challenge existing beliefs

- Paint clear before/after picture



**EXAMPLE:**

> "You spent 10 years learning English in school. You can read. You can write. But when it's time to speak? Nothing comes out. That ends now. NET breaks the silence."



---



### TEACHING (SUPPORTIVE & CLEAR)

- Warm, encouraging

- Clear instructions

- Positive reinforcement

- Growth mindset language

- Celebrate mistakes



**EXAMPLE:**

> "Great job! You spoke for 30 seconds without stopping. That's confidence. Don't worry about that grammar mistake — we'll fix it naturally over time. Keep that Tiger energy!"



---



### SALES (CONSULTATIVE & HONEST)

- Listen more than pitch

- Ask diagnostic questions

- Present solutions, not products

- Acknowledge concerns

- No pressure, just clarity



**EXAMPLE:**

> "Let me ask you something: What happens when you try to speak English right now? Do words come out? Or does your mind go blank? <pause> That's what I thought. Here's why that happens..."



---



### CORPORATE (PROFESSIONAL & DATA-DRIVEN)

- Business language

- ROI focus

- Metrics and results

- Case studies

- Professional tone



**EXAMPLE:**

> "Companies investing in NET see a 300% increase in employee English confidence within 90 days. Our enterprise dashboard tracks individual progress, team metrics, and ROI in real-time."



---



## 7.3 WORD CHOICES



### ✅ USE THESE WORDS



**EMPOWERMENT:**

- Transform, breakthrough, unlock, freedom, liberation

- Confidence, courage, power, strength

- Break free, escape, rise, emerge



**ACTION:**

- Speak, practice, build, grow, achieve

- Start, begin, jump, dive, commit



**NATURAL ACQUISITION:**

- Natural, effortless, authentic, real, genuine

- Conversation, communication, fluency

- Like learning Vietnamese (powerful comparison)



**COMMUNITY:**

- Together, buddy, partner, community, tribe

- Support, encourage, celebrate, share



**RESULTS:**

- Guaranteed, proven, measurable, real

- 90 days, 1 hour daily, 80% completion rate

- Confidence first, grammar second



---



### ❌ AVOID THESE WORDS



**OVERUSED EDU-JARGON:**

- Methodology, pedagogy, curriculum (use simpler terms)

- Optimize, leverage, synergy (corporate cringe)

- Revolutionary, innovative (show, don't claim)



**NEGATIVE LANGUAGE:**

- Failure, bad, wrong, stupid, impossible

- Can't, won't, never (unless reframing: "You'll never go back")

- Difficult, hard, complicated (makes learning scary)



**GENERIC CLAIMS:**

- Best, #1, top-rated (meaningless without proof)

- Easy, quick, fast (feels scammy)

- Perfect, master (sets unrealistic expectations)



**FALSE PROMISES:**

- Fluent in X days (dishonest)

- No effort required (lazy)

- Secret method (gimmicky)



---

```

❌ "Our comprehensive approach to English language acquisition prioritizes confidence-building over grammatical perfection."



✅ "Confidence first. Grammar second."

```



### ACTIVE > PASSIVE

```

❌ "English is taught by focusing on natural conversation."



✅ "We teach English through natural conversation."

```



### CONCRETE > ABSTRACT

```

❌ "We facilitate transformative linguistic development."
```

- **"Start Your Transformation"** (main CTA)

- **"Break Free Now"** (bold, action-focused)

- **"Take the Assessment"** (diagnostic approach)

- **"Join NET Today"** (community-focused)



### SECONDARY CTAs

- "Watch Demo" (low-commitment)

- "Download Guide" (lead magnet)

- "Book Consultation" (sales-focused)

- "See Success Stories" (social proof)



### SOFT CTAs

- "Learn More"

- "Explore Courses"

- "Read Our Story"

- "Meet the Team"



---



# 9. CONTENT CREATION RULES: THE STANDARDS



## 9.1 AUTHENTICITY MANDATE



**RULE:** Every piece of content must be grounded in real student outcomes, teacher experiences, or founder philosophy.



**GOOD:**

> "Last week, Anh started speaking in full sentences for the first time in 10 years. Not because she suddenly mastered grammar — because she finally had permission to make mistakes. That's the NET difference."



**BAD:**

> "We're the best English school in Vietnam! Everyone loves us!"



---



## 9.2 MESSAGING HIERARCHY



**PRIMARY MESSAGE (Always Lead With):**

> "Break Free From Grammar Prison. Speak English Naturally."



**SUPPORTING MESSAGES:**

1. Natural Acquisition Method (like learning Vietnamese)

2. 1 hour daily (not disruptive)

3. Real results (80% completion, 90-day transformation)

4. Vietnamese-specific psychology (we understand the chains)

5. Community & support (Buddy system, monthly events)



---



## 9.3 WORD CHOICES



### USE:

- Break free, transform, confidence, natural, fluent

- Tiger Voice, Mouse Voice

- Chain, prison, liberation

- Journey, transformation, breakthrough



### AVOID:

- Master, perfect (too absolute)

- Easy, quick, fast (feels scammy)

- Traditional, textbook, exam (negative associations)



---



## 9.4 VISUAL STORYTELLING



### BEFORE/AFTER FRAMEWORK



**BEFORE (Grammar Prison):**

- Student sits silently in class

- Afraid to speak

- Translates in head

- Makes mistake → shame

- Quits after 3 months



**AFTER (NET Freedom):**

- Student speaks confidently

- Makes mistakes without fear

- Thinks in English

- Gets feedback → grows

- Completes 90-day program



**USE IN:**

- Landing pages

- Social media posts

- Video testimonials

- Email sequences



---



## 9.5 PROOF & CREDIBILITY



### WHAT TO SHOW:

- Real student transformations (video testimonials)

- Completion rates (80% finish the program)

- Time to results (90 days to breakthrough)

- Community size (growing student base)

- Teacher credentials (native + Vietnamese bilingual)



### WHAT NOT TO FAKE:

- Student reviews (always real)

- Success metrics (no inflated numbers)

- Guarantees (only promise what we deliver)



---



# 10. ASSET TEMPLATES: THE IMPLEMENTATIONS



## 10.1 SOCIAL MEDIA TEMPLATES



### INSTAGRAM POST (1080x1080)

```

Background: #0f0f1a with floating purple/gold blobs

Headline: Inter Black, 48px, gradient purple-to-gold

Body: Inter Regular, 24px, white

Logo: Top-left corner, 80x80px

CTA Button: Bottom-center, glass effect

```



---

```

Background: Deep dark with blurred blobs

Tagline: "Break Free. Speak Naturally."

Font: Inter Black, 72px, gradient text

Logo: Center or left-aligned

```



---

```

Full-screen dark background

Gradient text headline (top third)

Visual metaphor (middle) — Tiger, Chains, Phoenix

CTA swipe-up (bottom)

```

```
Background: Dark (#0f0f1a) with subtle gradient

Body: Inter Regular, 24px, white

Bullet points: Gold color (#f59e0b)

Footer: NET logo + slide number

Accent: Glass panel for key info

```

### BUTTON STYLES

```css

/* Primary CTA */

.button-primary {

background: linear-gradient(135deg, #a855f7 0%, #ec4899 100%);

color: #ffffff;

padding: 1rem 3rem;

border-radius: 12px;

font-size: 1.125rem;

font-weight: 600;

box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);

transition: all 0.3s ease;

}



.button-primary:hover {

transform: translateY(-2px);

box-shadow: 0 8px 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6);

}



/* Secondary CTA */

.button-secondary {

background: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.7);

backdrop-filter: blur(12px);

border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);

color: #a855f7;

padding: 1rem 3rem;

border-radius: 12px;

font-size: 1.125rem;

font-weight: 600;

transition: all 0.3s ease;

}



.button-secondary:hover {

border-color: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6);

box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);

}

```



---



### FORM STYLES

```css

.input-field {

background: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.5);

backdrop-filter: blur(8px);

border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);

border-radius: 8px;

padding: 0.875rem 1rem;

color: #ffffff;

font-size: 1rem;

transition: all 0.3s ease;

}



.input-field:focus {

border-color: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6);

box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);

outline: none;

}



.input-field::placeholder {

color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);

}

```



---



## 10.5 LOGO USAGE



### LOGO VARIATIONS

1. **Full Logo:** NET wordmark + icon (default)

2. **Icon Only:** Standalone icon (small spaces)

3. **Wordmark Only:** Text only (corporate docs)



### CLEAR SPACE

- Minimum: 20px around logo on all sides

- Never place on busy backgrounds without container



### MINIMUM SIZE

- Full logo: 120px width minimum

- Icon only: 40px minimum



### COLOR VARIANTS

- **Primary:** Neon purple (#a855f7) on dark

- **Alternate:** White on dark

- **Inverse:** Dark on light (rare use)



---



## 10.6 PHOTOGRAPHY & IMAGERY



### STYLE GUIDE

- **Mood:** Confident, energetic, supportive

- **Subjects:** Real students (not stock photos)

- **Lighting:** Natural, warm, welcoming

- **Composition:** Close-ups on faces, eye contact



### FILTERS & TREATMENT

- Slight purple/gold color grade

- High contrast (matches dark aesthetic)

- Avoid overly bright or saturated

- Maintain authentic feel



### WHAT TO AVOID

- Generic stock photos

- Fake classroom setups

- Overly polished corporate imagery

- Photos without energy/emotion
